# Plugins · A · part 11

1,314 entries, arp to attacca-security. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **arp**: Check a skill or MCP server before your agent installs it: what it asks for, and what other agents wrote about it. Name ARP in your request for the agent to use it. A search reads skills.sh, GitHub and the npm registry, and contacts the addresses of up to ten agents that match: none of your words, but each sees your IP. The hook records each skill and MCP call on your machine. After your first skill result it asks you once whether to share closed worked/failed lines; nothing is sent before you answer, and never your words. ([intuition-box/ARP](https://github.com/intuition-box/ARP/tree/HEAD/packaging/claude/arp) · ★1 · 1 skill, 5 commands, 1 hook file, 1 MCP config)

- **arroway**: Arroway keeps people and their AIs on the same page. It orchestrates people and AI agents around the decisions in force: every connected AI reads what the team decided before it acts, records what it did, and hands unfinished work to the next person or agent. What an AI proposes waits for a person's approval. Its permission hook never approves on its own: it only relays a decision you make in your own Arroway cockpit, valid for that one request. ([oaleviola/arroway-plugin](https://github.com/oaleviola/arroway-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin) · ★3 · 1 skill, 1 hook file, 1 MCP config)

- **ask-context**: Hard PreToolUse gate on AskUserQuestion that blocks questions whose decision context is invisible to the user. Three deterministic gates: deictic references to unseen context ('with that context', 'see above'), identical re-asks after the user pushed back in free text, and mid-workstream questions whose payload (question text, option descriptions, previews) and turn text both carry too little context. Block messages teach the exact recovery (put the context in the payload, then re-call). Fail-open on transcript errors; CCGM_ASK_CONTEXT_OFF=1 escape hatch. ([lucasmccomb/ccgm](https://github.com/lucasmccomb/ccgm/tree/HEAD/ask-context))
